Google 계정당 1회만 실행하면 되고, 이후 `setup-sheets-sync` 가 저장된 자격증명을 재사용한다. 브라우저 자동화(예: Claude in Chrome)는 하드 전제다 — 스킬은 맨 처음 활성화 여부를 확인하고 꺼져 있으면 중간까지 진행하지 않고 즉시 중단한다. `gcloud` CLI는 설치돼 있으면 결정적 단계를 위한 선택적 가속기일 뿐이다. ([jhs512/ib](https://github.com/jhs512/ib/tree/HEAD/skills/ib/setup-gcp) · ★1)

Offers three paths: the Deepgram CLI MCP proxy (dg mcp), the standalone deepgram-mcp package, and the credential-free hosted documentation MCP. Use whenever someone wants to install Deepgram's agentic tools, set up the MCP server, or connect their editor to Deepgram. ([deepgram/skills](https://github.com/deepgram/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/setup-mcp) · ★29)
- **setup-mcp**: Configures developer environment -- MCP servers, tokens, and permissions ([asysta-act/agent-flow](https://github.com/asysta-act/agent-flow/tree/HEAD/skills/setup-mcp) · ★12)
- **setup-mcp**: Configure .mcp.json with MCP servers for external services your project uses. Called by init or standalone to configure the MCP pillar. Trigger phrases: "setup mcp", "configure mcp servers", "connect to [service]". ([ShipWithAI/shipwithai-plugins](https://github.com/ShipWithAI/shipwithai-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/starter/skills/setup-mcp) · ★9)
